QuantyraNullCone.finite_realizer_rank_rigidity {n m : ℕ} {u v : Fin n → ℝ} {r : ℝ} (hn : 0 < n) (hm : 16 ≤ m)
  (hr : 0 < r) (hmr : ↑m * r = 1) (uDistinct : Function.Injective u) (vDistinct : Function.Injective v)
  (occ : QuantyraNullCone.GridOccupied u v m r) (L : QuantyraNullCone.Realizer (QuantyraNullCone.Chronological u v))
  (margin : ∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → t ≤ 1 → |QuantyraNullCone.marginalCDF v t - t| ≤ 2 * r)
  (boundaryBound : ↑(QuantyraNullCone.boundary u v r).card ≤ 20 * r * ↑n) :
  ∃ swap,
    ∀ (i : Fin n),
      QuantyraNullCone.Interior u v r i →
        |↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank (L.aligned swap).first i) - ↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank (fun x y => u x < u y) i)| ≤
            30 * r * ↑n ∧
          |↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank (L.aligned swap).second i) - ↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank (fun x y => v x < v y) i)| ≤
            30 * r * ↑n
QuantyraNullCone.finite_realizer_rank_rigidity_specified {n m : ℕ} {u v : Fin n → ℝ} {r : ℝ} (hn : 1 ≤ n) (hm : 16 ≤ m)
  (rValue : r = 1 / ↑m) (_inUnit : ∀ (i : Fin n), 0 < u i ∧ u i < 1 ∧ 0 < v i ∧ v i < 1)
  (uDistinct : Function.Injective u) (vDistinct : Function.Injective v)
  (_noGridLines : ∀ (i : Fin n) (k : ℕ), u i ≠ ↑k * r ∧ v i ≠ ↑k * r) (occ : QuantyraNullCone.GridOccupied u v m r)
  (L : QuantyraNullCone.Realizer (QuantyraNullCone.Chronological u v))
  (_marginU : ∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → t ≤ 1 → |QuantyraNullCone.marginalCDF u t - t| ≤ 2 * r)
  (marginV : ∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → t ≤ 1 → |QuantyraNullCone.marginalCDF v t - t| ≤ 2 * r)
  (boundaryFraction : ↑(QuantyraNullCone.boundary u v r).card / ↑n ≤ 20 * r) :
  ∃ swap,
    ∀ (i : Fin n),
      QuantyraNullCone.Interior u v r i →
        |↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank (L.aligned swap).first i) - ↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank (fun x y => u x < u y) i)| ≤
            30 * r * ↑n ∧
          |↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank (L.aligned swap).second i) - ↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank (fun x y => v x < v y) i)| ≤
            30 * r * ↑n
QuantyraNullCone.finite_realizer_coordinate_rigidity {n m : ℕ} {u v : Fin n → ℝ} {r : ℝ} (hn : 0 < n) (hm : 16 ≤ m)
  (hr : 0 < r) (hmr : ↑m * r = 1) (uDistinct : Function.Injective u) (vDistinct : Function.Injective v)
  (occ : QuantyraNullCone.GridOccupied u v m r) (L : QuantyraNullCone.Realizer (QuantyraNullCone.Chronological u v))
  (marginU : ∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → t ≤ 1 → |QuantyraNullCone.marginalCDF u t - t| ≤ 2 * r)
  (marginV : ∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → t ≤ 1 → |QuantyraNullCone.marginalCDF v t - t| ≤ 2 * r)
  (boundaryBound : ↑(QuantyraNullCone.boundary u v r).card ≤ 20 * r * ↑n) :
  ∃ swap,
    ∀ (i : Fin n),
      QuantyraNullCone.Interior u v r i →
        |↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank (L.aligned swap).first i) / ↑n - u i| ≤ 32 * r ∧
          |↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank (L.aligned swap).second i) / ↑n - v i| ≤ 32 * r
QuantyraNullCone.finite_realizer_cumulative_error {n m : ℕ} {u v : Fin n → ℝ} {r : ℝ} (hn : 0 < n) (hm : 16 ≤ m)
  (hr : 0 < r) (hmr : ↑m * r = 1) (uDistinct : Function.Injective u) (vDistinct : Function.Injective v)
  (occ : QuantyraNullCone.GridOccupied u v m r) (L : QuantyraNullCone.Realizer (QuantyraNullCone.Chronological u v))
  (marginU : ∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → t ≤ 1 → |QuantyraNullCone.marginalCDF u t - t| ≤ 2 * r)
  (marginV : ∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → t ≤ 1 → |QuantyraNullCone.marginalCDF v t - t| ≤ 2 * r)
  (boundaryBound : ↑(QuantyraNullCone.boundary u v r).card ≤ 20 * r * ↑n) (F : ℝ → ℝ → ℝ)
  (thresholdStability : ∀ (s t s' t' : ℝ), |F s t - F s' t'| ≤ |s - s'| + |t - t'|)
  (latentAccuracy : ∀ (s t : ℝ), |QuantyraNullCone.empiricalCDF u v s t - F s t| ≤ 3 * r) :
  ∃ swap,
    ∀ (s t : ℝ),
      |QuantyraNullCone.empiricalCDF (fun i => ↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank (L.aligned swap).first i) / ↑n)
              (fun i => ↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank (L.aligned swap).second i) / ↑n) s t -
            F s t| ≤
        87 * r

QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.indicator_concentration {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) {S : Set QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint}
  (hS : MeasurableSet S) {r : ℝ} (hr : 0 ≤ r) :
  (QuantyraNullCone.sampleMeasure rho n).real
      {sample |
        r ≤ |(∑ i, S.indicator (fun x => 1) (sample i)) / ↑n - ((QuantyraNullCone.densityMeasure rho) S).toReal|} ≤
    2 * Real.exp (-2 * ↑n * r ^ 2)
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.empiricalCDF_concentration {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (s t : ℝ) {r : ℝ} (hr : 0 ≤ r) :
  (QuantyraNullCone.sampleMeasure rho n).real
      {sample |
        r ≤
          |QuantyraNullCone.empiricalCDF (fun i => (sample i).1) (fun i => (sample i).2) s t -
              QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho s t|} ≤
    2 * Real.exp (-2 * ↑n * r ^ 2)
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.grid_vertices_failure {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (m : ℕ) {r : ℝ} (hr : 0 ≤ r) :
  (QuantyraNullCone.sampleMeasure rho n).real
      {sample | ¬QuantyraNullCone.GridVertexAccuracy (fun i => (sample i).1) (fun i => (sample i).2) rho m r} ≤
    2 * (↑m + 1) ^ 2 * Real.exp (-2 * ↑n * r ^ 2)
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.sample_good_probability {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) {n m : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) {r : ℝ} (hr : 0 < r) (hmr : ↑m * r = 1) :
  1 - (↑m ^ 2 * Real.exp (-↑n * r ^ 2 / 2) + 2 * (↑m + 1) ^ 2 * Real.exp (-2 * ↑n * r ^ 2)) ≤
    (QuantyraNullCone.sampleMeasure rho n).real {sample | QuantyraNullCone.SampleGood rho m r sample}
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.reconstruction_probability_grid {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) {n m : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (hm : 16 ≤ m) {r : ℝ} (hr : 0 < r)
  (hmr : ↑m * r = 1) :
  1 - (↑m ^ 2 * Real.exp (-↑n * r ^ 2 / 2) + 2 * (↑m + 1) ^ 2 * Real.exp (-2 * ↑n * r ^ 2)) ≤
    (QuantyraNullCone.sampleMeasure rho n).real {sample | QuantyraNullCone.SampleCDFReconstructed rho (87 * r) sample}
QuantyraNullCone.grid_failure_bound {n m : ℕ} {x r : ℝ} (hx : 16 ≤ x) (hmx : ↑m ≤ x) (hmr : ↑m * r = 1)
  (hPower : x ^ 4 = ↑n) : ↑m ^ 2 * Real.exp (-↑n * r ^ 2 / 2) + 2 * (↑m + 1) ^ 2 * Real.exp (-2 * ↑n * r ^ 2) ≤ 3 / 32
QuantyraNullCone.fourth_root_grid_data {n : ℕ} (hn : 65536 ≤ n) :
  have x := √√↑n;
  have m := ⌊x⌋₊;
  have r := 1 / ↑m;
  16 ≤ x ∧ 16 ≤ m ∧ 0 < r ∧ ↑m * r = 1 ∧ ↑m ≤ x ∧ x ^ 4 = ↑n ∧ 87 * r ≤ 174 / x
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.reconstruction_probability {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) {n : ℕ} (hn : 65536 ≤ n) :
  9 / 10 ≤
    (QuantyraNullCone.sampleMeasure rho n).real
      {sample | QuantyraNullCone.SampleCDFReconstructed rho (174 * ↑n ^ (-1 / 4)) sample}

QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.rectangle_mass_cdf {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) {a b c d : ℝ} (hab : a ≤ b) (hcd : c ≤ d) :
  (QuantyraNullCone.densityMeasure rho).real (Set.Ioc a b ×ˢ Set.Ioc c d) =
    QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho b d - QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho a d -
        QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho b c +
      QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho a c
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.coefficientDeviation_attained {rho sigma : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hS : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass sigma) :
  ∃ p ∈ QuantyraNullCone.diamond, QuantyraNullCone.coefficientDeviation rho sigma = |rho p - sigma p|
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.coefficient_interpolation {rho sigma : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hS : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass sigma) {epsilon : ℝ}
  (hCDF : ∀ (s t : ℝ), |QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho s t - QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF sigma s t| ≤ epsilon) :
  QuantyraNullCone.coefficientDeviation rho sigma ^ 3 ≤ 2048 * epsilon

  (h : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) {n : ℕ} (hn : 65536 ≤ n) :
  9 / 10 ≤ (QuantyraNullCone.orderLaw rho n).real {code | QuantyraNullCone.OrderCDFGood rho (174 * ↑n ^ (-1 / 4)) code}
QuantyraNullCone.finite_probability_event_bound.{u_1} {α : Type u_1} [Fintype α] [MeasurableSpace α]
  [MeasurableSingletonClass α] (μ ν : MeasureTheory.Measure α) [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
  [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure ν] (S : Set α) : μ.real S - ν.real S ≤ 1 / 2 * ∑ x, |μ.real {x} - ν.real {x}|
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.orderLawTV_event_bound {rho sigma : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hS : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass sigma) (n : ℕ)
  (S : Set (QuantyraNullCone.OrderCode n)) :
  (QuantyraNullCone.orderLaw rho n).real S - (QuantyraNullCone.orderLaw sigma n).real S ≤
    QuantyraNullCone.orderLawTV rho sigma n
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.orderCDFGood_intersects {rho sigma : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hS : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass sigma) {n : ℕ} (hn : 65536 ≤ n)
  (hTV : QuantyraNullCone.orderLawTV rho sigma n < 4 / 5) :
  ∃ code,
    QuantyraNullCone.OrderCDFGood rho (174 * ↑n ^ (-1 / 4)) code ∧
      QuantyraNullCone.OrderCDFGood sigma (174 * ↑n ^ (-1 / 4)) code
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.populationCDF_orbit_of_orderLawTV {rho sigma : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hS : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass sigma) {n : ℕ} (hn : 65536 ≤ n)
  (hTV : QuantyraNullCone.orderLawTV rho sigma n < 4 / 5) :
  (∀ (s t : ℝ),
      |QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho s t - QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF sigma s t| ≤ 348 * ↑n ^ (-1 / 4)) ∨
    ∀ (s t : ℝ),
      |QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho s t - QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF sigma t s| ≤ 348 * ↑n ^ (-1 / 4)

  MeasureTheory.MeasurePreserving QuantyraNullCone.transposePoint QuantyraNullCone.diamondVolume
    QuantyraNullCone.diamondVolume
QuantyraNullCone.densityMeasure_transpose_apply (rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ)
  {S : Set QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint} (hS : MeasurableSet S) :
  (QuantyraNullCone.densityMeasure (QuantyraNullCone.transposeDensity rho)) S =
    (QuantyraNullCone.densityMeasure rho) (QuantyraNullCone.transposePoint ⁻¹' S)

  QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF (QuantyraNullCone.transposeDensity rho) s t = QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho t s

  QuantyraNullCone.coefficientDeviation (QuantyraNullCone.transposeDensity rho)
      (QuantyraNullCone.transposeDensity sigma) =
    QuantyraNullCone.coefficientDeviation rho sigma
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.coefficientDeviation_bounds {rho sigma : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hS : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass sigma) :
  0 ≤ QuantyraNullCone.coefficientDeviation rho sigma ∧ QuantyraNullCone.coefficientDeviation rho sigma ≤ 1
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.conformalDistance_bounds {rho sigma : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hS : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass sigma) :
  0 ≤ QuantyraNullCone.conformalDistance rho sigma ∧ QuantyraNullCone.conformalDistance rho sigma ≤ 1
QuantyraNullCone.orderLawTV_le_finiteLawDiscrepancy (rho sigma : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ) {k N : ℕ}
  (hk : 2 ≤ k) (hN : k ≤ N) :
  QuantyraNullCone.orderLawTV rho sigma k ≤ QuantyraNullCone.finiteLawDiscrepancy rho sigma N
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.conformalDistance_of_small_TV {rho sigma : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hS : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass sigma) {n : ℕ} (hn : 65536 ≤ n)
  (hTV : QuantyraNullCone.orderLawTV rho sigma n < 4 / 5) :
  QuantyraNullCone.conformalDistance rho sigma ≤ 100 * ↑n ^ (-1 / 12)
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.full_inverse {rho sigma : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hS : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass sigma) {N : ℕ} (hN : 2 ≤ N) :
  QuantyraNullCone.conformalDistance rho sigma ≤
    100 * (↑N ^ (-1 / 12) + QuantyraNullCone.finiteLawDiscrepancy rho sigma N)
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.coefficientDeviation_zero_iff {rho sigma : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hS : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass sigma) :
  QuantyraNullCone.coefficientDeviation rho sigma = 0 ↔ ∀ p ∈ QuantyraNullCone.diamond, rho p = sigma p
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.conformalDistance_zero_iff {rho sigma : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hS : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass sigma) :
  QuantyraNullCone.conformalDistance rho sigma = 0 ↔
    (∀ p ∈ QuantyraNullCone.diamond, rho p = sigma p) ∨
      ∀ p ∈ QuantyraNullCone.diamond, rho p = QuantyraNullCone.transposeDensity sigma p

  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hS : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass sigma)
  (hLaw : ∀ (n : ℕ), 2 ≤ n → QuantyraNullCone.orderLaw rho n = QuantyraNullCone.orderLaw sigma n) :
  (∀ p ∈ QuantyraNullCone.diamond, rho p = sigma p) ∨
    ∀ p ∈ QuantyraNullCone.diamond, rho p = QuantyraNullCone.transposeDensity sigma p

QuantyraNullCone.sampledOrder_strict_partial_order {n : ℕ} (sample : Fin n → QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint) :
  (∀ (i : Fin n), ¬QuantyraNullCone.CodeRelation (QuantyraNullCone.sampledOrder sample) i i) ∧
    ∀ (i j k : Fin n),
      QuantyraNullCone.CodeRelation (QuantyraNullCone.sampledOrder sample) i j →
        QuantyraNullCone.CodeRelation (QuantyraNullCone.sampledOrder sample) j k →
          QuantyraNullCone.CodeRelation (QuantyraNullCone.sampledOrder sample) i k
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.sample_relabel_preserving {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) {n : ℕ} (π : Equiv.Perm (Fin n)) :
  MeasureTheory.MeasurePreserving (⇑(QuantyraNullCone.relabelSample π)) (QuantyraNullCone.sampleMeasure rho n)
    (QuantyraNullCone.sampleMeasure rho n)
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.orderLaw_exchangeable {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) {n : ℕ} (π : Equiv.Perm (Fin n)) :
  MeasureTheory.Measure.map (QuantyraNullCone.relabelOrder π) (QuantyraNullCone.orderLaw rho n) =
    QuantyraNullCone.orderLaw rho n
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.orderLaw_singleton_relabel {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) {n : ℕ} (π : Equiv.Perm (Fin n)) (code : QuantyraNullCone.OrderCode n) :
  (QuantyraNullCone.orderLaw rho n) {QuantyraNullCone.relabelOrder π code} = (QuantyraNullCone.orderLaw rho n) {code}
QuantyraNullCone.abs_sum_of_constant.{u_1} {α : Type u_1} (s : Finset α) (g : α → ℝ)
  (hConst : ∀ x ∈ s, ∀ y ∈ s, g x = g y) : |∑ x ∈ s, g x| = ∑ x ∈ s, |g x|
QuantyraNullCone.finite_map_L1_of_fiber_constant.{u_1, u_2} {α : Type u_1} {β : Type u_2} [Fintype α] [Fintype β]
  [MeasurableSpace α] [MeasurableSingletonClass α] [MeasurableSpace β] [MeasurableSingletonClass β]
  (μ ν : MeasureTheory.Measure α) [MeasureTheory.IsFiniteMeasure μ] [MeasureTheory.IsFiniteMeasure ν] (f : α → β)
  (hμ : ∀ (x y : α), f x = f y → μ.real {x} = μ.real {y}) (hν : ∀ (x y : α), f x = f y → ν.real {x} = ν.real {y}) :
  ∑ b, |(MeasureTheory.Measure.map f μ).real {b} - (MeasureTheory.Measure.map f ν).real {b}| =
    ∑ x, |μ.real {x} - ν.real {x}|
QuantyraNullCone.forgetOrderLabels_eq_iff {n : ℕ} (code other : QuantyraNullCone.OrderCode n) :
  QuantyraNullCone.forgetOrderLabels code = QuantyraNullCone.forgetOrderLabels other ↔
    ∃ π, QuantyraNullCone.relabelOrder π code = other

QuantyraNullCone.log_large_bounds {n : ℕ} (hn : 65536 ≤ n) : 1 ≤ Real.log ↑n ∧ 2048 * Real.log ↑n ≤ ↑n
QuantyraNullCone.logarithmic_grid_data {n : ℕ} (hn : 65536 ≤ n) :
  have x := √(↑n / (8 * Real.log ↑n));
  have m := ⌊x⌋₊;
  have r := 1 / ↑m;
  16 ≤ m ∧ 0 < r ∧ ↑m * r = 1 ∧ ↑m ^ 2 ≤ ↑n / (8 * Real.log ↑n) ∧ 87 * r ≤ 174 / x
QuantyraNullCone.logarithmic_grid_failure {n m : ℕ} (hn : 65536 ≤ n) (hm : 16 ≤ m) {r : ℝ} (hmr : ↑m * r = 1)
  (hmSq : ↑m ^ 2 ≤ ↑n / (8 * Real.log ↑n)) :
  ↑m ^ 2 * Real.exp (-↑n * r ^ 2 / 2) + 2 * (↑m + 1) ^ 2 * Real.exp (-2 * ↑n * r ^ 2) ≤ 1 / 10
QuantyraNullCone.logarithmic_grid_reciprocal {n : ℕ} (hn : 2 ≤ n) :
  1 / √(↑n / (8 * Real.log ↑n)) = √8 * √(Real.log ↑n / ↑n)
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.logarithmic_reconstruction_probability {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) {n : ℕ} (hn : 65536 ≤ n) :
  9 / 10 ≤
    (QuantyraNullCone.sampleMeasure rho n).real
      {sample | QuantyraNullCone.SampleCDFReconstructed rho (174 * √8 * √(Real.log ↑n / ↑n)) sample}
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.logarithmic_orderCDFGood_probability {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) {n : ℕ} (hn : 65536 ≤ n) :
  9 / 10 ≤
    (QuantyraNullCone.orderLaw rho n).real
      {code | QuantyraNullCone.OrderCDFGood rho (174 * √8 * √(Real.log ↑n / ↑n)) code}
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.logarithmic_populationCDF_orbit_of_orderLawTV
  {rho sigma : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ} (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho)
  (hS : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass sigma) {n : ℕ} (hn : 65536 ≤ n)
  (hTV : QuantyraNullCone.orderLawTV rho sigma n < 4 / 5) :
  (∀ (s t : ℝ),
      |QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho s t - QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF sigma s t| ≤
        348 * √8 * √(Real.log ↑n / ↑n)) ∨
    ∀ (s t : ℝ),
      |QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho s t - QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF sigma t s| ≤
        348 * √8 * √(Real.log ↑n / ↑n)
QuantyraNullCone.logarithmic_small_sample_lower {n : ℕ} (hn : 2 ≤ n) (hSmall : n < 65536) :
  1 ≤ 130 * (Real.log ↑n / ↑n) ^ (1 / 6)

    130 * ((Real.log ↑N / ↑N) ^ (1 / 6) + QuantyraNullCone.finiteLawDiscrepancy rho sigma N)

QuantyraNullCone.sqrt_coefficient_lipschitz {a b : ℝ} (ha : 1 / 2 ≤ a) (hb : 1 / 2 ≤ b) :
  |√(2 * a) - √(2 * b)| ≤ |a - b|
QuantyraNullCone.FutureCurve.integral_derivU {p q : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint}
  (c : QuantyraNullCone.FutureCurve p q) : ∫ (t : ℝ), deriv c.u t ∂QuantyraNullCone.curveMeasure = q.1 - p.1
QuantyraNullCone.FutureCurve.integral_derivV {p q : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint}
  (c : QuantyraNullCone.FutureCurve p q) : ∫ (t : ℝ), deriv c.v t ∂QuantyraNullCone.curveMeasure = q.2 - p.2
QuantyraNullCone.FutureCurve.integral_weight_cauchy_schwarz {p q : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint}
  (c : QuantyraNullCone.FutureCurve p q) :
  ∫ (t : ℝ), c.weight t ∂QuantyraNullCone.curveMeasure ≤ √(q.1 - p.1) * √(q.2 - p.2)
QuantyraNullCone.FutureCurve.integrable_length {p q : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint}
  (c : QuantyraNullCone.FutureCurve p q) {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun t => √(2 * rho (c.point t) * deriv c.u t * deriv c.v t)) QuantyraNullCone.curveMeasure
QuantyraNullCone.FutureCurve.length_difference {p q : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint}
  (c : QuantyraNullCone.FutureCurve p q) {rho sigma : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hS : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass sigma) :
  |c.length rho - c.length sigma| ≤ QuantyraNullCone.coefficientDeviation rho sigma
QuantyraNullCone.timeSeparation_empty (rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ) (p q : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint)
  [IsEmpty (QuantyraNullCone.FutureCurve p q)] : QuantyraNullCone.timeSeparation rho p q = 0
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.timeSeparation_difference {rho sigma : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hS : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass sigma)
  (p q : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint) :
  |QuantyraNullCone.timeSeparation rho p q - QuantyraNullCone.timeSeparation sigma p q| ≤
    QuantyraNullCone.coefficientDeviation rho sigma
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.timeSeparation_transpose {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (p q : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint) :
  QuantyraNullCone.timeSeparation (QuantyraNullCone.transposeDensity rho) p q =
    QuantyraNullCone.timeSeparation rho (QuantyraNullCone.transposePoint p) (QuantyraNullCone.transposePoint q)
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.proper_time_conformalDistance {rho sigma : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hS : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass sigma) :
  ∃ swap,
    ∀ (p q : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint),
      |QuantyraNullCone.timeSeparation rho p q -
            QuantyraNullCone.timeSeparation sigma (QuantyraNullCone.alignedPoint swap p)
              (QuantyraNullCone.alignedPoint swap q)| ≤
        QuantyraNullCone.conformalDistance rho sigma
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.proper_time_inverse_unlabeled {rho sigma : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hS : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass sigma) {N : ℕ} (hN : 2 ≤ N) :
  ∃ swap,
    ∀ (p q : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint),
      |QuantyraNullCone.timeSeparation rho p q -
            QuantyraNullCone.timeSeparation sigma (QuantyraNullCone.alignedPoint swap p)
              (QuantyraNullCone.alignedPoint swap q)| ≤
        100 * (↑N ^ (-1 / 12) + QuantyraNullCone.unlabeledFiniteLawDiscrepancy rho sigma N)
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.proper_time_logarithmic_unlabeled {rho sigma : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hS : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass sigma) {N : ℕ} (hN : 2 ≤ N) :
  ∃ swap,
    ∀ (p q : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint),
      |QuantyraNullCone.timeSeparation rho p q -
            QuantyraNullCone.timeSeparation sigma (QuantyraNullCone.alignedPoint swap p)
              (QuantyraNullCone.alignedPoint swap q)| ≤
        130 * ((Real.log ↑N / ↑N) ^ (1 / 6) + QuantyraNullCone.unlabeledFiniteLawDiscrepancy rho sigma N)

QuantyraNullCone.residual_box_bound {r x L U : ℝ} (hL : L ≤ x) (hU : x ≤ U) : min (r * L) (r * U) ≤ r * x
QuantyraNullCone.finite_lp_weak_duality {n m k : ℕ} (A : Fin m → Fin n → ℝ) (E : Fin k → Fin n → ℝ) (b : Fin m → ℝ)
  (d : Fin k → ℝ) (c x L U : Fin n → ℝ) (y : Fin m → ℝ) (z : Fin k → ℝ) (hA : ∀ (i : Fin m), ∑ j, A i j * x j ≤ b i)
  (hE : ∀ (i : Fin k), ∑ j, E i j * x j = d i) (hY : ∀ (i : Fin m), y i ≤ 0) (hL : ∀ (j : Fin n), L j ≤ x j)
  (hU : ∀ (j : Fin n), x j ≤ U j) :
  ∑ i, b i * y i + ∑ i, d i * z i +
      ∑ j, min ((c j - ∑ i, A i j * y i - ∑ i, E i j * z i) * L j) ((c j - ∑ i, A i j * y i - ∑ i, E i j * z i) * U j) ≤
    ∑ j, c j * x j
QuantyraNullCone.rationalDual_checker_sound {n m k : ℕ} (A : Fin m → Fin n → ℚ) (E : Fin k → Fin n → ℚ) (b : Fin m → ℚ)
  (d : Fin k → ℚ) (c L U : Fin n → ℚ) (y : Fin m → ℚ) (z : Fin k → ℚ) (x : Fin n → ℝ)
  (hValid : QuantyraNullCone.rationalDualValid y = true) (hA : ∀ (i : Fin m), ∑ j, ↑(A i j) * x j ≤ ↑(b i))
  (hE : ∀ (i : Fin k), ∑ j, ↑(E i j) * x j = ↑(d i)) (hL : ∀ (j : Fin n), ↑(L j) ≤ x j)
  (hU : ∀ (j : Fin n), x j ≤ ↑(U j)) : ↑(QuantyraNullCone.rationalDualLower A E b d c L U y z) ≤ ∑ j, ↑(c j) * x j
QuantyraNullCone.Realizer.originalForceStep_sound {n : ℕ} {rows : Fin n → Fin n → Bool}
  (L : QuantyraNullCone.Realizer (QuantyraNullCone.rowRelation rows)) (parent arc : QuantyraNullCone.FiniteArc n)
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.originalForceStep rows parent arc = true) :
  L.opposite parent.1 parent.2 ↔ L.opposite arc.1 arc.2
QuantyraNullCone.Realizer.checkForcingTrace_sound {n : ℕ} {rows : Fin n → Fin n → Bool}
  (L : QuantyraNullCone.Realizer (QuantyraNullCone.rowRelation rows)) (anchor : QuantyraNullCone.FiniteArc n)
  (trace : List (QuantyraNullCone.ForcingEntry n))
  (hAccept : QuantyraNullCone.checkForcingTrace rows anchor trace = true) (arc : QuantyraNullCone.FiniteArc n) :
  arc ∈ List.map Prod.fst trace → (L.opposite arc.1 arc.2 ↔ L.opposite anchor.1 anchor.2)
QuantyraNullCone.Realizer.forcing_trace_global_alignment {n : ℕ} {rows : Fin n → Fin n → Bool}
  (L R : QuantyraNullCone.Realizer (QuantyraNullCone.rowRelation rows)) (anchor : QuantyraNullCone.FiniteArc n)
  (trace : List (QuantyraNullCone.ForcingEntry n))
  (hAccept : QuantyraNullCone.checkForcingTrace rows anchor trace = true) :
  ∃ swap, ∀ arc ∈ List.map Prod.fst trace, (L.aligned swap).opposite arc.1 arc.2 ↔ R.opposite arc.1 arc.2
QuantyraNullCone.Realizer.checked_forcing_rank_bounds {n : ℕ} {rows : Fin n → Fin n → Bool}
  (L R : QuantyraNullCone.Realizer (QuantyraNullCone.rowRelation rows)) (anchor : QuantyraNullCone.FiniteArc n)
  (trace : List (QuantyraNullCone.ForcingEntry n))
  (hAccept : QuantyraNullCone.checkForcingTrace rows anchor trace = true) :
  ∃ swap,
    ∀ (i : Fin n),
      |↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank (L.aligned swap).first i) - ↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank R.first i)| ≤
          ↑(QuantyraNullCone.unresolvedDegree rows trace i) ∧
        |↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank (L.aligned swap).second i) - ↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank R.second i)| ≤
          ↑(QuantyraNullCone.unresolvedDegree rows trace i)
QuantyraNullCone.negative_exp_upper_512 {t : ℝ} (ht : 0 ≤ t) : Real.exp (-t) ≤ 1 / (1 + t / 512) ^ 512
QuantyraNullCone.negative_exp_upper_rational {t : ℚ} (ht : 0 ≤ t) :
  Real.exp (-↑t) ≤ ↑(QuantyraNullCone.negativeExpUpperQ t)
QuantyraNullCone.roundFailureQ_upper {D : ℕ} (hD : 0 < D) (raw : ℚ) : raw ≤ QuantyraNullCone.roundFailureQ D raw
QuantyraNullCone.roundFailureQ_le_budget {raw budget : ℚ} (hRaw : raw ≤ budget) :
  QuantyraNullCone.roundFailureQ (10 ^ 12 * budget.den) raw ≤ budget
QuantyraNullCone.rounded_split_failure_budget {rawM rawJ budgetM budgetJ delta : ℚ} (hM : rawM ≤ budgetM)
  (hJ : rawJ ≤ budgetJ) (hSplit : budgetM + budgetJ = delta) :
  QuantyraNullCone.roundFailureQ (10 ^ 12 * budgetM.den) rawM +
      QuantyraNullCone.roundFailureQ (10 ^ 12 * budgetJ.den) rawJ ≤
    delta
QuantyraNullCone.finite_grid_CDF {n q : ℕ} {u v : Fin n → ℝ} {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ} {r eta : ℝ}
  (hK : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hr : 0 < r) (hqr : ↑q * r = 1) (hEta : 0 ≤ eta)
  (hU : ∀ (i : Fin n), 0 ≤ u i ∧ u i ≤ 1) (hV : ∀ (i : Fin n), 0 ≤ v i ∧ v i ≤ 1)
  (hGrid :
    ∀ (j k : ℕ),
      j ≤ q →
        k ≤ q →
          |QuantyraNullCone.empiricalCDF u v (↑j * r) (↑k * r) - QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho (↑j * r) (↑k * r)| ≤
            eta)
  (s t : ℝ) : |QuantyraNullCone.empiricalCDF u v s t - QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho s t| ≤ eta + 2 * r
QuantyraNullCone.normalized_rank_coordinate_error_general {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) {w : Fin n → ℝ}
  (distinct : Function.Injective w) {L : Fin n → Fin n → Prop} {i : Fin n} {budget epsilon : ℝ}
  (hRank : |↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank L i) - ↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank (fun x y => w x < w y) i)| ≤ budget)
  (hMargin : |QuantyraNullCone.marginalCDF w (w i) - w i| ≤ epsilon) :
  |↑(QuantyraNullCone.rank L i) / ↑n - w i| ≤ budget / ↑n + epsilon
QuantyraNullCone.checked_trimmed_CDF {n q : ℕ} {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hK : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) (hn : 0 < n) (rows : Fin n → Fin n → Bool) (u v : Fin n → ℝ)
  (hu : Function.Injective u) (hv : Function.Injective v)
  (hRows : ∀ (i j : Fin n), QuantyraNullCone.rowRelation rows i j ↔ QuantyraNullCone.Chronological u v i j)
  (hU : ∀ (i : Fin n), 0 ≤ u i ∧ u i ≤ 1) (hV : ∀ (i : Fin n), 0 ≤ v i ∧ v i ≤ 1)
  (L : QuantyraNullCone.Realizer (QuantyraNullCone.rowRelation rows)) (anchor : QuantyraNullCone.FiniteArc n)
  (trace : List (QuantyraNullCone.ForcingEntry n))
  (hAccept : QuantyraNullCone.checkForcingTrace rows anchor trace = true) {epsilonM epsilonJ r : ℝ} (hM : 0 ≤ epsilonM)
  (hJ : 0 ≤ epsilonJ) (hr : 0 < r) (hqr : ↑q * r = 1)
  (hMU : ∀ (i : Fin n), |QuantyraNullCone.marginalCDF u (u i) - u i| ≤ epsilonM)
  (hMV : ∀ (i : Fin n), |QuantyraNullCone.marginalCDF v (v i) - v i| ≤ epsilonM)
  (hGrid :
    ∀ (j k : ℕ),
      j ≤ q →
        k ≤ q →
          |QuantyraNullCone.empiricalCDF u v (↑j * r) (↑k * r) - QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho (↑j * r) (↑k * r)| ≤
            epsilonJ) :
  ∃ swap,
    ∀ (cutoff : ℕ) (s t : ℝ),
      |(L.aligned swap).rankCDF s t - QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho s t| ≤
        ↑(QuantyraNullCone.trimTailCount rows trace cutoff) / ↑n + 2 * ↑cutoff / ↑n + 2 * epsilonM + epsilonJ + 2 * r
QuantyraNullCone.rank_position_eq {n : ℕ} (positions : Fin n → Fin n) (hInjective : Function.Injective positions)
  (i : Fin n) : QuantyraNullCone.rank (fun j k => positions j < positions k) i = ↑(positions i) + 1
QuantyraNullCone.checkedPositionRealizer_ranks {n : ℕ} (rows : Fin n → Fin n → Bool) (x y : Fin n → Fin n)
  (hAccept : QuantyraNullCone.checkPositionRealizer rows x y = true) (i : Fin n) :
  QuantyraNullCone.rank (QuantyraNullCone.checkedPositionRealizer rows x y hAccept).first i = ↑(x i) + 1 ∧
    QuantyraNullCone.rank (QuantyraNullCone.checkedPositionRealizer rows x y hAccept).second i = ↑(y i) + 1

QuantyraNullCone.dkw_log_ratio_lower {z : ℝ} (hz : 0 ≤ z) (hz1 : z < 1) : 2 * z ≤ Real.log ((1 + z) / (1 - z))
QuantyraNullCone.dkw_log_ratio_upper {z : ℝ} (hz : 0 ≤ z) (hz1 : z < 1) :
  Real.log ((1 + z) / (1 - z)) ≤ 2 * z / (1 - z ^ 2)
QuantyraNullCone.dkw_stationarity_root {e : ℝ} (he : 0 < e) (he1 : e < 1) :
  ∃ p, (1 - e) / 2 ≤ p ∧ p ≤ 1 / 2 ∧ p < 1 - e ∧ QuantyraNullCone.dkwStationarity e p = 0
QuantyraNullCone.dkw_bernoulli_pinsker {p q : ℝ} (hp : 0 < p) (hpq : p ≤ q) (hq1 : q < 1) :
  2 * (q - p) ^ 2 ≤ q * Real.log (q / p) + (1 - q) * Real.log ((1 - q) / (1 - p))

  HasDerivAt (QuantyraNullCone.dkwLikelihoodDerivative e lambda) (QuantyraNullCone.dkwLikelihoodSecond e lambda t) t
QuantyraNullCone.dkw_likelihood_stationary_value_lower {e p : ℝ} (he : 0 < e) (hp : 0 < p) (hpe : p < 1 - e) :
  2 * e ^ 2 ≤ QuantyraNullCone.dkwLikelihoodBarrier e (e / (1 - p - e)) p
QuantyraNullCone.dkw_sharp_likelihood_barrier {e : ℝ} (he : 0 < e) (he1 : e < 1) :
  ∃ lambda, 0 < lambda ∧ ∀ (t : ℝ), 0 < t → t ≤ 1 - e → 2 * e ^ 2 ≤ QuantyraNullCone.dkwLikelihoodBarrier e lambda t

QuantyraNullCone.finite_bin_atom_card {n q k : ℕ} (hkq : k ≤ q) (x : QuantyraNullCone.BinProfile n q) :
  (QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinAtom k x).card = k ^ QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinCount x k
QuantyraNullCone.finite_bin_atom_likelihood_identity {n q k : ℕ} (hk : 0 < k) (hkq : k ≤ q) (lambda : ℝ)
  (x : QuantyraNullCone.BinProfile n q) :
  ∑ y ∈ QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinAtom k x, QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinLikelihood lambda 1 y =
    ↑(QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinAtom k x).card * QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinLikelihood lambda k x
QuantyraNullCone.finite_bin_terminal_sum {n q : ℕ} {lambda : ℝ} (hq : 0 < q) (hL : 0 < lambda) :
  ∑ x, QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinLikelihood lambda 1 x = ↑q ^ n
QuantyraNullCone.finite_bin_reveal_count {n q k j : ℕ} {x y : QuantyraNullCone.BinProfile n q}
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinReveal k x = QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinReveal k y) (hkj : k ≤ j) :
  QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinCount x j = QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinCount y j
QuantyraNullCone.finite_bin_invariant_terminal_sum {n q k : ℕ} (hk : 0 < k) (hkq : k ≤ q) (lambda : ℝ)
  (E : Finset (QuantyraNullCone.BinProfile n q))
  (hE :
    ∀ (x y : QuantyraNullCone.BinProfile n q),
      QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinReveal k x = QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinReveal k y → (x ∈ E ↔ y ∈ E)) :
  ∑ x ∈ E, QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinLikelihood lambda 1 x = ∑ x ∈ E, QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinLikelihood lambda k x
QuantyraNullCone.finite_bin_likelihood_maximal {n q : ℕ} {lambda B : ℝ} (hq : 0 < q) (hL : 0 < lambda) (hB : 0 < B) :
  ↑(QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinCrossing lambda B).card / ↑q ^ n ≤ 1 / B
QuantyraNullCone.finite_bin_deviation_likelihood {n q k : ℕ} {e lambda : ℝ} (hn : 0 < n) (hq : 0 < q) (hk : 0 < k)
  (hL : 0 < lambda) (x : QuantyraNullCone.BinProfile n q)
  (hBarrier : ∀ (t : ℝ), 0 < t → t ≤ 1 - e → 2 * e ^ 2 ≤ QuantyraNullCone.dkwLikelihoodBarrier e lambda t)
  (hBad : e < QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinCDF x k - ↑k / ↑q) :
  Real.exp (2 * ↑n * e ^ 2) < QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinLikelihood lambda k x
QuantyraNullCone.finite_bin_upper_DKW {n q : ℕ} {e : ℝ} (hn : 0 < n) (hq : 0 < q) (he : 0 < e) (he1 : e < 1) :
  ↑(QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinUpperDeviation e).card / ↑q ^ n ≤ Real.exp (-2 * ↑n * e ^ 2)
QuantyraNullCone.finite_bin_reflect_CDF {n q k : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (hq : 0 < q) (hkq : k ≤ q)
  (x : QuantyraNullCone.BinProfile n q) :
  QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinCDF (QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinReflect x) (q - k) - ↑(q - k) / ↑q =
    ↑k / ↑q - QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinCDF x k
QuantyraNullCone.finite_bin_two_sided_DKW {n q : ℕ} {e : ℝ} (hn : 0 < n) (hq : 0 < q) (he : 0 ≤ e) :
  ↑(QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinAbsDeviation e).card / ↑q ^ n ≤ 2 * Real.exp (-2 * ↑n * e ^ 2)

QuantyraNullCone.uniform_quantized_CDF {n q k : ℕ} (hq : 0 < q) (w : Fin n → ℝ)
  (hw : ∀ (i : Fin n), w i ∈ Set.Ioc 0 1) :
  QuantyraNullCone.finiteBinCDF (QuantyraNullCone.uniformQuantizedProfile q hq w) k =

QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.split_checked_sample_CDF {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) {n q : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) (hq : 0 < q) {eM eJ : ℝ} (hM : 0 ≤ eM) (hJ : 0 ≤ eJ)
  (w : Fin n → QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint) (hSquare : ∀ (i : Fin n), w i ∈ QuantyraNullCone.diamond)
  (hu : Function.Injective fun i => (w i).1) (hv : Function.Injective fun i => (w i).2)
  (hGood : w ∉ QuantyraNullCone.splitCalibrationBad rho n q eM eJ)
  (L : QuantyraNullCone.Realizer (QuantyraNullCone.rowRelation (QuantyraNullCone.sampledOrder w)))
  (anchor : QuantyraNullCone.FiniteArc n) (trace : List (QuantyraNullCone.ForcingEntry n))
  (hAccept : QuantyraNullCone.checkForcingTrace (QuantyraNullCone.sampledOrder w) anchor trace = true) :
  ∃ swap,
    ∀ (cutoff : ℕ) (s t : ℝ),
      |(L.aligned swap).rankCDF s t - QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho s t| ≤
        ↑(QuantyraNullCone.trimTailCount (QuantyraNullCone.sampledOrder w) trace cutoff) / ↑n + 2 * ↑cutoff / ↑n +
              2 * eM +
            eJ +
          2 / ↑q

QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.cell_mean_integral {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) {a c w : ℝ} (hw : 0 ≤ w) (ha : 0 ≤ a) (hc : 0 ≤ c) (haw : a + w ≤ 1)
  (hcw : c + w ≤ 1) :
  QuantyraNullCone.densityCellMean rho a c w = (∫ (x : ℝ) in a..a + w, ∫ (y : ℝ) in c..c + w, rho (x, y)) / w ^ 2
'QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.cell_mean_integral' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.cell_mean_bounds {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) {a c w : ℝ} (hw : 0 < w) (ha : 0 ≤ a) (hc : 0 ≤ c) (haw : a + w ≤ 1)
  (hcw : c + w ≤ 1) :
  1 / 2 ≤ QuantyraNullCone.densityCellMean rho a c w ∧ QuantyraNullCone.densityCellMean rho a c w ≤ 3 / 2

QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.cell_mean_CDF {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) {a c w : ℝ} (hw : 0 ≤ w) :
  QuantyraNullCone.densityCellMean rho a c w =
    (QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho (a + w) (c + w) - QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho a (c + w) -
          QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho (a + w) c +
        QuantyraNullCone.populationCDF rho a c) /
      w ^ 2
'QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.cell_mean_CDF' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

    F p q - F 0 q - F p 0 + F 0 0

        QuantyraNullCone.histogramGridCDF coefficient k i j)

QuantyraNullCone.check_density_LP_model_sound {n k : ℕ} (R : QuantyraNullCone.CDFReport n)
  (A : Fin (QuantyraNullCone.densityInequalityRows k).length → Fin (k * k) → ℚ)
  (E : Fin (QuantyraNullCone.densityEqualityRows k).length → Fin (k * k) → ℚ)
  (b : Fin (QuantyraNullCone.densityInequalityRows k).length → ℚ)
  (d : Fin (QuantyraNullCone.densityEqualityRows k).length → ℚ)
  (h : QuantyraNullCone.checkDensityLPModel R k A E b d = true) :
  A = QuantyraNullCone.densityLP_A k ∧
    E = QuantyraNullCone.densityLP_E k ∧ b = QuantyraNullCone.densityLP_b R k ∧ d = QuantyraNullCone.densityLP_d k

QuantyraNullCone.iid_indicator_chernoff.{u_1} {Ω : Type u_1} [MeasurableSpace Ω] {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure Ω}
  [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ] {n : ℕ} (hn : 0 < n) {S : Set Ω} (hS : MeasurableSet S) {t : ℝ} (ht0 : 0 ≤ t)
  (ht1 : t ≤ 1) (eps : ℝ) :

      {sample | eps ≤ |(∑ i, S.indicator (fun x => 1) (sample i)) / ↑n - μ.real S|} ≤

QuantyraNullCone.degree_inner_mesh_data {m : ℕ} (hm : 65536 ≤ m) :
  have r := 1 / ↑m;
  have h := √r;
  have k := ⌊√↑m⌋₊;
  have w := (1 - 16 * h) / ↑k;
  0 < r ∧
    0 < h ∧
      h ^ 2 = r ∧
        ↑m * r = 1 ∧ h ≤ 1 / 256 ∧ 0 < k ∧ ↑k ^ 2 ≤ ↑m ∧ 0 < w ∧ ↑k * w = 1 - 16 * h ∧ 15 / 16 * h ≤ w ∧ w ≤ 2 * h

QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.fourth_root_density_estimation {rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ}
  (hK : QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho) {n : ℕ} (hn : 2 ≤ n) :
  19 / 20 ≤
    (QuantyraNullCone.unlabeledOrderLaw rho n).real
      {code |
        QuantyraNullCone.DensityEstimateGood rho (QuantyraNullCone.degreeRadius n)
          (QuantyraNullCone.selectedDegreeDensity code)}
'QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass.fourth_root_density_estimation' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]
QuantyraNullCone.fourth_root_order_only_estimator {n : ℕ} (hn : 2 ≤ n) :
  ∃ estimate,
    Measurable estimate ∧
      (∀ (code : QuantyraNullCone.UnlabeledOrderCode n), Measurable (estimate code)) ∧
        (∀ (code : QuantyraNullCone.UnlabeledOrderCode n) (p : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint),
            1 / 2 ≤ estimate code p ∧ estimate code p ≤ 3 / 2) ∧
          ∀ (rho : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ),
            QuantyraNullCone.InDensityClass rho →
              19 / 20 ≤
                (QuantyraNullCone.unlabeledOrderLaw rho n).real
                  {code |
                    QuantyraNullCone.DensityEstimateGood rho (min (1 / 2) (650 * (Real.log ↑n / ↑n) ^ (1 / 4)))
                      (estimate code)}
'QuantyraNullCone.fourth_root_order_only_estimator' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
QuantyraNullCone.density_estimate_common_orbit {rho sigma f : QuantyraNullCone.DiamondPoint → ℝ} {E : ℝ}
  (hR : QuantyraNullCone.DensityEstimateGood rho E f) (hS : QuantyraNullCone.DensityEstimateGood sigma E f) :
  (∀ p ∈ QuantyraNullCone.diamond, |rho p - sigma p| ≤ 2 * E) ∨
    ∀ p ∈ QuantyraNullCone.diamond, |rho p - QuantyraNullCone.transposeDensity sigma p| ≤ 2 * E

    ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, QuantyraNullCone.lowerProfile h x ^ 2 ≤ h * ∫ (t : ℝ), QuantyraNullCone.oddGaussian t ^ 2

  [MeasurableSingletonClass α] (μ ν : MeasureTheory.Measure α) [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
  [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure ν] (a : α → ℝ) (ha : ∀ (i : α), 0 ≤ a i ∧ a i ≤ 1) :
